| Rui | Yes. It seems solid up through the middle, but we're having trouble with the ending. | talk |

| KAITO | I'll gladly join your discussion.<br>Can you tell me what kind of story you're trying to tell? | talk |
| Tsukasa | Yes. Our protagonist is an introverted girl who plans to spend her spring break by just reading. | talk |
| Tsukasa | However, her parents have quite a lot to prepare as they're about to welcome a new family member into the world. | talk |
| Tsukasa | For the time being, the girl is sent off to the countryside to live with her uncle and his wife. | talk |
| Rui | But she has trouble adjusting to this new environment. Not only that, she starts feeling lonely because there aren't any other children her age in this village. | talk |
| Rui | As a result, she's driven towards trying to find her way back to her beloved parents all on her own. | talk |
| Rui | As she ventures up the mountain on her uncle's property, she comes across a girl her age named Some sitting under a cherry blossom tree. | talk |
| KAITO | Some...?<br>Does this cherry blossom tree happen to be a Somei-yoshino? | talk |
| Tsukasa | Yes, exactly!<br>Some's actually a cherry blossom spirit. | talk |
| Tsukasa | As such, she's able to conjure up cherry blossoms from her hands and even cause petal blizzards. | talk |
| Tsukasa | Our protagonist is drawn towards Some's wondrous powers and they soon become friends. | talk |
| Rui | But as they grow closer, we're thinking that she'll begin figuring out Some's true identity. | talk |
| Rui | After Some and the protagonist become friends, they promise to meet again the next day and she heads back to her uncle's home. | talk |
| Rui | Her meeting Some also results in being able to open up to her uncle more. | talk |
| Rui | But one night, her uncle goes out to gather some herbs from the mountain and shows no signs of returning. | talk |
| KAITO | I see. What happens next? | talk |
| Tsukasa | The protagonist begins blaming herself for saying, “I want to try those herbs again.” Thus, she feels responsible and goes out searching for him. | talk |
| Tsukasa | However, the night is no time for a girl to be searching a mountain alone, and she soon becomes lost as a result. | talk |
| Rui | Just as she's about to cry, our cherry blossom spirit, Some, appears. | talk |
| Rui | She decided to help search for the protagonist's uncle by calling out to her Somei-yoshino siblings who also live in the mountains. | talk |
| KAITO | Oh, that's right. I've heard a story that Somei-yoshino cherry blossoms originate from just one tree. | talk |
| Rui | Yes.<br>That's how we plan on presenting the idea of “siblings” during our show. | talk |
| Rui | Hehe. Emu will be playing the role of Some, and I'm thinking about having multiple copies of her during this scene. | talk |

| Tsukasa | I still don't understand how he plans on doing that, but let's continue... | talk |
| Rui | Ultimately, the location of the protagonist's injured uncle will be revealed thanks to Some's help. | talk |
| Rui | And a path lined with cherry blossom petals will guide them back to the villagers who've been searching for them. | talk |
| KAITO | Yes, that sounds like a very moving climax. | talk |
| Tsukasa | I agree. I believe we have a solid story up to this point. | talk |
| Tsukasa | However, we're having trouble with the scene where Some and the protagonist have to say goodbye to each other. | talk |

| Miku | Oh... | talk |
| KAITO | Come to think of it, he might be right... | talk |
| Shiho | I mean, the fact that it's growing from the platform of a train station is already weird. | talk |
|  | bgm00024 | Sound |
| Ichika | Maybe we should have replanted it somewhere with more space when it was a little smaller... | talk |
| Honami | But it sprung up here all on its own, so it might be right where it needs to be. | talk |
| KAITO | Right... | talk |
| KAITO | You could try replanting it, but discover that it's returned to its original spot after a little while... That seems entirely possible. | talk |
| Saki | Wait a second!<br>Shouldn't we worry more about the roof than the tree? | talk |
| Shiho | Wait, why? | talk |
| Saki | Remember how it had a huge growth spurt after absorbing those black fragments of feelings? | talk |
| Saki | If it can grow that fast, then next time it happens it might blast the roof wide open! | talk |
| KAITO | It's true, their feelings are incredibly strong. | talk |
| KAITO | And the tree grows based on those feelings, so it might start pressing up against the roof soon. | talk |
| KAITO | I'm sure that this SEKAI isn't about to get in the way of these girls growing as a band... | talk |
| KAITO | Hehe. Now I'm looking forward to seeing this tree's next growth spurt. | talk |
| Rin's Voice | Hey, you guys!<br>What's the holdup?! | talk |
| MEIKO's Voice | It's time to start practice! | talk |
| Miku | Oh, right...<br>We were here to get KAITO. | talk |
| Ichika | *Nod*<br>Let's go, KAITO. We're looking forward to practicing with you again today. | talk |
| KAITO | Me too... | talk |
|  | se_walk_women_001_4 | Sound |
| KAITO | Since I'm here, I think I'll watch them practice a little. | talk |
| Saki | So, what do we do if the tree does break the station's roof? | talk |
| Shiho | You're still stuck on that? | talk |
| Honami | Hehe. If that happens, we may need to come over and help clean everything up. | talk |
| Saki | I hadn't thought of that!<br>That sounds really hard, so let's forget the whole thing! | talk |
| KAITO | Hehe. | talk |
|  | bgm00000 | Sound |
